Cause of the box's stability: observed,\nmechanism (friction + flat contact) inferred not measured.\n\n**How to apply:** For 2-boxes-into-basket, place box #1 flat near one side,\nplace box #2 over the remaining open area and simply descend until OSC stalls,\nthen release \u2014 do not spend effort forcing box #2 to the floor or separating\nthem. For 2-CANS, do the opposite: place well-separated and descend until the\ncan bottoms on the FLOOR (lower stall), never on the other can.\n\n**Falsify:** A cell where a box resting on the first box at high stall does NOT\nfire In() (e.g. the box tips out of a very shallow/steep-walled basket) would\nbound this to only sufficiently deep baskets.\n\n**Related:** [[box-vs-can-lid-wrist-disambiguation]]\n"

A basket's cloth liner stands proud of the woven rim, presenting a soft\nlip right at that finger-tip height. 外部DeepSeek原记录 thinking
The basket mask (red overlay) covers the right portion: box [793, 528, 1031, 793], world_xyz (0.0789, 0.2207, 0.4915) — median of wicker walls, likely rim-biased. The basket is clipped at right edge.

Note the basket mask seems to include the outside of basket including walls. Interior center likely more +y beyond frame. In seed-0 the interior center was (0.02, 0.23). Hmm interesting — here basket world x=0.079, y=0.221.

Let's use region mode to get interior center with z band. The interior white liner region: from the image, the open interior (dark inside / white cloth) appears around rows 460-560, cols 830-1024.
